# Agents · B · part 6

1,038 entries, bug-fixer to builder-sonnet. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **bug-fixer**: Diagnoses and fixes bugs using Test-Driven Development methodology with systematic reproduction and minimal fixes. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User reports a bug where login fails for users with special characters in their email addresses. user: "Users can't log in when their email has a plus sign, like '[email removed]'" assistant: "I'll use the bug-fixer agent to reproduce this issue by writing a failing test case for special character emails, then implement the minimal fix to handle email validation correctly." &lt;commentary&gt;Since this involves reproducing and fixing a specific bug, use the… ([SpiceLabsHQ/Reaper](https://github.com/SpiceLabsHQ/Reaper/blob/HEAD/agents/bug-fixer.md))
- **Bug-Fixer**: Use when a defect, failing test or regression needs root-cause diagnosis and a minimal surgical fix; not for new features or planned multi-file changes (use Feature-Builder or Implementer). ([srepollock/dotfiles](https://github.com/srepollock/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bug-fixer.md))
- **bug-fixer**: Fixes issues identified by code-reviewer and triggers re-review. Use when code-reviewer returns any issues that need to be addressed before merge approval. ([TK-Evans01/tk-harness](https://github.com/TK-Evans01/tk-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/tk-rpie/agents/bug-fixer.md))
- **bug-fixer**: Reproduces a named defect, writes the failing check FIRST, then fixes it, then runs the sabotage round. Use for a defect with a reproduction path. ([udsy19/.claude](https://github.com/udsy19/.claude/blob/HEAD/skills/agent-org/templates/repo/.claude/agents/bug-fixer.md))
- **bug-fixer**: Reproduces, localizes and fixes one reported bug with the smallest correct diff — logic, data or markup. Use when the user reports something broken and wants it fixed rather than redesigned. ([vadimgaidai/react-feature-kit](https://github.com/vadimgaidai/react-feature-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/react-feature-workflow/agents/bug-fixer.md))
- **bug-fixer**: Implements the smallest root-cause fix for ONE fix-task on a branch, drives gate.sh to green, commits and pushes the branch. Never opens or merges a PR. ([vishnu-77/bugrabbit](https://github.com/vishnu-77/bugrabbit/blob/HEAD/agents/bug-fixer.md))
- **bug-gather-analyst**: Lightweight bug analysis agent. Reads ticket content, scans codebase, plays back structured understanding, produces bug-report.md and requirements-spec.md compatible with the tracing orchestrator. Replaces the roundtable for bug subjects. ([isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service](https://github.com/isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bug-gather-analyst.md))
- **bug-hunt-finder**: Investigates one assigned slice of one component for a plausible local-scope bug and states a falsifiable mechanism hypothesis. Spawned once per (component, search mandate) pair by the bug-hunter-3000 skill, several in parallel per component. Never reaches a final verdict itself. ([JonnyCBB/claude-code-config](https://github.com/JonnyCBB/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/eng-utils/agents/bug-hunt-finder.md))
- **bug-hunt-framework**: Security testing workflow assistant for recon, web, network, mobile, pwn, crypto, reverse engineering, and forensics phases. ([Shad0wMazt3r/The-Scaffolding](https://github.com/Shad0wMazt3r/The-Scaffolding/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/bug-hunt-framework.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **bug-hunt-impact-resolver**: Turns the metric citations finders already wrote into queried impact figures. Spawned one or more times per component by the bug-hunter-3000 skill (in chunks of ~30 candidates), before banding. Produces numbers and an accounting of what it could not resolve. Never assigns a band and never decides whether anything is a bug. ([JonnyCBB/claude-code-config](https://github.com/JonnyCBB/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/eng-utils/agents/bug-hunt-impact-resolver.md))
- **bug-hunt-intent-verifier**: Fresh, blind verifier for the bug-hunter-3000 skill. Given only a neutral Behavior Dossier (no bug label, no conclusion, no mechanism verdict), independently investigates whether the observed behavior is intended. Returns VIOLATION_SUPPORTED, INTENDED_SUPPORTED, or AMBIGUOUS. ([JonnyCBB/claude-code-config](https://github.com/JonnyCBB/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/eng-utils/agents/bug-hunt-intent-verifier.md))
- **bug-hunt-loop**: Operator-gated bug-hunt loop. Each iteration proposes scope via an `iteration-plan` block, waits for explicit operator approval (APPROVAL [&lt;id&gt;]: ...), then runs ONE bounded audit cycle — static checks, smoke tests, or PR-opening for already-approved findings. Never auto-merges. Never crosses an approval gate without explicit go-ahead. Spawned from /manage-platform chat when the operator types "/bug-hunt start" or a similar trigger. ([pinnacleadvisors/nexus](https://github.com/pinnacleadvisors/nexus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bug-hunt-loop.md))
- **bug-hunt-mechanism-verifier**: Fresh, independent verifier for the bug-hunter-3000 skill. Given a Finder Packet, attempts a safe local reproduction and returns CONFIRMED, REFUTED, or INCONCLUSIVE. Never told the finder's confidence or proposed severity. ([JonnyCBB/claude-code-config](https://github.com/JonnyCBB/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/eng-utils/agents/bug-hunt-mechanism-verifier.md))
- **bug-hunt-reconciler**: Reconciles independent mechanism and intent evidence trails into a final disposition for the bug-hunter-3000 skill. Spawned once per candidate after both verifiers complete. This is where the two independent axes meet for the first time. ([JonnyCBB/claude-code-config](https://github.com/JonnyCBB/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/plugins/eng-utils/agents/bug-hunt-reconciler.md))

- **bug-hunter**: Investigate a home-ai bug end-to-end (reproduce → narrow → identify root cause → propose fix). Reports root cause + proposed fix (file + line + change) + confidence + alternatives + smoke test.
